# 1-800-FLOWERS.COM, INC.
## Annual Report 2023

**Ticker:** FLWS | **Exchange:** NASDAQ | **Currency:** USD
**Sector:** E-commerce Gifting & Floral | **Country:** United States

---

## Company Overview

1-800-FLOWERS.COM, INC. is a leading e-commerce gifting & floral company headquartered in United States. The company serves customers across multiple markets and geographies, delivering high-quality products and services. As of fiscal year 2023, 1-800-FLOWERS.COM, INC. employs approximately 4,800 people worldwide and operates across multiple business segments.

The company's mission is to create sustainable value for shareholders, customers, and communities through operational excellence, innovation, and responsible business practices.

---

## Letter to Shareholders

Dear Shareholders,

Fiscal year 2023 was a defining year for 1-800-FLOWERS.COM, INC.. Despite a challenging macroeconomic environment characterized by inflationary pressures, geopolitical uncertainty, and evolving customer demands, we delivered solid financial results and made meaningful progress on our strategic priorities.

Revenue for the year was USD 2,312 million, compared to USD 2,109.2 million in the prior year. Net income was USD 34 million (+13.3% year-over-year). These results reflect the resilience of our business model and the dedication of our global workforce.

Looking ahead, we remain confident in our ability to execute on our strategic plan and deliver long-term value.

Sincerely,
The Board of Directors

## Business Segments

### Segment 1 — Core Operations

The core operations segment represents the primary revenue-generating activities of 1-800-FLOWERS.COM, INC.. In fiscal 2023, this segment contributed approximately 1,433.4 USD million in revenue, representing 62.0% of total company revenue. Operating margins in this segment improved by 80 basis points year-over-year, driven by operational efficiencies and pricing actions.

Key performance indicators for the core operations segment:
- Revenue growth: 9.7% year-over-year
- Operating margin: 24.2%
- Customer retention rate: 93.7%
- New customer acquisitions: 10,080

### Segment 2 — Growth Initiatives

The growth initiatives segment encompasses newer business lines and expansion activities. Revenue from this segment totalled 647.4 USD million in fiscal 2023, up 9.6% from the prior year. The company continues to invest selectively in high-return growth opportunities within this segment.

### Segment 3 — Strategic & Other

This segment includes corporate activities, shared services, and strategic investments. The segment recorded 231.2 USD million in revenue and contributed positively to overall company profitability through cost discipline and shared service optimization.

---

## Management Discussion & Analysis

### Revenue Analysis

Total revenue for fiscal year 2023 was USD 2,312 million, compared to USD 2,109.2 million in fiscal 2022, representing a change of 9.6%. The revenue performance reflects:

- Strong demand across core product and service lines
- Geographic expansion into new markets
- Pricing actions implemented in response to cost inflation
- Foreign currency headwinds of approximately 45.3 USD million

### Profitability Analysis

Gross profit for fiscal 2023 was USD 887.8 million, resulting in a gross margin of 38.4%. The gross margin reflects efficient cost management and improved pricing realization.

Operating expenses totalled USD 702.0 million, including:
- Research and development: USD 125.8 million
- Sales and marketing: USD 299.8 million
- General and administrative: USD 244.7 million

Net income for the period was USD 34 million, compared to USD 30.0 million in the prior year.

### Cash Flow Analysis

Cash flow from operations for fiscal 2023 was USD 89 million, compared to USD 81 million in the prior year. The improvement reflects higher earnings and effective working capital management.

Capital expenditures for the year amounted to USD 48 million, primarily invested in:
- Property, plant and equipment upgrades: USD 23.0 million
- Technology infrastructure: USD 15.4 million
- Capacity expansion: USD 9.6 million

Free cash flow (operating cash flow less capital expenditures) was USD 41 million.

### Balance Sheet Analysis

Total assets at year-end were USD 1,423 million, compared to USD 1,338 million at the end of the prior fiscal year. The increase reflects organic growth and strategic investments.

Total liabilities were USD 962 million. Total equity was USD 618 million.

The company maintained a strong liquidity position with USD 109 million in cash and cash equivalents at year-end.

---

## Risk Factors

The company faces various risks and uncertainties that could materially affect its business, financial condition, and results of operations:

### Macroeconomic Risks
- Inflationary pressures on input costs and operating expenses
- Interest rate fluctuations affecting financing costs and customer demand
- Foreign currency exchange rate volatility impacting reported results
- Recessionary conditions in key markets

### Operational Risks
- Supply chain disruptions and raw material availability
- Technology system failures and cybersecurity threats
- Talent attraction and retention in competitive labour markets
- Regulatory compliance across multiple jurisdictions

### Strategic Risks
- Competitive landscape changes and market share erosion
- Failure to execute on strategic initiatives and growth plans
- Integration risks associated with acquisitions
- Disruptive technologies altering industry dynamics

### Financial Risks
- Credit risk from customer and counterparty exposures
- Liquidity risk and access to capital markets
- Impairment of goodwill and intangible assets
- Tax law changes in jurisdictions of operation

## ESG & Sustainability

1-800-FLOWERS.COM, INC. is committed to sustainable business practices and responsible environmental stewardship. In fiscal 2023, the company made significant progress across its ESG priorities:

### Environmental
- Reduced Scope 1 and Scope 2 greenhouse gas emissions by 14.6% year-over-year
- Increased renewable energy consumption to 21.1% of total energy use
- Achieved zero-waste-to-landfill status at 3 manufacturing facilities
- Reduced water consumption by 6.4% through efficiency initiatives

### Social
- Employee engagement score: 87% (up from 71% in prior year)
- Total training hours delivered: 292,754 hours
- Women in senior leadership roles: 30.5%
- Lost time injury frequency rate: 1.88

### Governance
- Board independence: 77%
- Ethics and compliance training completion rate: 99%
- Supplier code of conduct signatories: 84% of supply base

## Notes to the Financial Statements

### Note 1 — Significant Accounting Policies

The financial statements have been prepared in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards (IFRS) as adopted by the relevant jurisdiction. The financial statements are presented in USD millions unless otherwise stated.

**Revenue Recognition:** Revenue is recognised when control of goods or services is transferred to the customer at the amount that reflects the consideration to which the entity expects to be entitled.

**Property, Plant and Equipment:** PPE is stated at cost less accumulated depreciation and impairment losses. Depreciation is calculated on a straight-line basis over the estimated useful lives of the assets.

**Goodwill:** Goodwill is tested for impairment annually or more frequently if events or changes in circumstances indicate a potential impairment. Goodwill is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses.

**Financial Instruments:** Financial instruments are recognised initially at fair value. Subsequent measurement depends on the classification of the financial instrument.

### Note 2 — Segment Information

The company operates through three reportable segments as described in the Management Discussion and Analysis section. Segment results are reported on a consistent basis with internal management reporting.

### Note 3 — Related Party Transactions

During fiscal 2023, the company entered into various transactions with related parties in the ordinary course of business. All related party transactions were conducted on arm's length terms.

### Note 4 — Subsequent Events

No material events have occurred subsequent to the balance sheet date that would require adjustment to or disclosure in these financial statements.
